  }, [assets.data?.items, selectedAssetIds, structuredContracts, truthKind])
  const selectedWorkflowIssue = selectedWorkflowVersion
    ? mechanismVersionStockIssue(selectedWorkflowVersion)
    : null
  const promptExecutionSummaries = selectedWorkflowVersion
    ? workflowPromptExecutionSummaries(selectedWorkflowVersion.manifest)
    : []
  const expectedRealRequests = selectedWorkflowVersion && selectedSet.data
    ? expectedPromptRequests(
        selectedWorkflowVersion.manifest,
        selectedSet.data.summary.item_count,
      )
    : 0
  // 费用要按模型计价算，只有服务端知道；上限也由服务端持有（可被环境变量调整）。
  // 前端不再自带上限副本，避免服务端调整后界面还在展示过期数字。
  // 预估请求带自己的世代号：切换开关后，上一次模式的迟到响应既不展示、也不作为启动依据。
  const realExecutionPreview = useRealExecutionEstimate({
    enabled: modelExecutionMode === "real"
      && selectedSetId > 0
      && typeof workflowVersionId === "number"
      && workflowVersionId > 0,
    baselineSetId: selectedSetId,
    workflowVersionId: typeof workflowVersionId === "number" ? workflowVersionId : 0,
    mode: inferenceMode,
    fetchEstimate: (request) => baselineRegressionApi.realExecutionPreview(
      request.baselineSetId,
      request.workflowVersionId,
      request.mode,
    ),
  })
  const realBudget = realExecutionPreview.preview ?? undefined
  const realEstimateReady = modelExecutionMode !== "real" || realBudget !== undefined
  const realRequestCapExceeded = modelExecutionMode === "real"
    && realBudget !== undefined
    && !realBudget.within_caps

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      workflowVersionId
      && !(stockWorkflowVersions.data ?? []).some(
        (item) => item.id === workflowVersionId,
      )
    ) {
      setWorkflowVersionId(0)
    }
  }, [stockWorkflowVersions.data, workflowVersionId])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      truthKind === "structured_fields"
      && truthContracts.data
      && !truthContracts.data.items.length
    ) {
      setTruthKind("level")
    }
  }, [truthContracts.data, truthKind])

  useEffect(() => {
    // Do not fight the loaded run's category with a stale category in its URL.
    const canonicalSearch = baselineRunRouteSearch(searchParams, selectedRunId, runDetail.data?.summary)
    if (canonicalSearch) {
      setSearchParams(canonicalSearch, { replace: true })
      return
    }
    const categoryFromUrl = searchParams.get("category_key")
    if (categoryFromUrl && categoryFromUrl !== selectedCategoryKey) {
      setSelectedCategoryKey(categoryFromUrl)
      setSelectedSetId(0)
      setSelectedRunId(0)
    }
  }, [searchParams, selectedCategoryKey, selectedRunId, runDetail.data?.summary, setSearchParams])

  useEffect(() => {
    // 地址已经变了，之后地址里再出现这次运行就是一次新的指定。
    if (leftUrlRun !== null && leftUrlRun.locationKey !== routeLocation.key) setLeftUrlRun(null)
  }, [leftUrlRun, routeLocation.key])

  useEffect(() => {
    // 列表在选中之后重新读到过了，以后按列表里有没有它来定。
    if (explicitSet !== null && explicitSet.listUpdatedAt !== baselineSets.dataUpdatedAt) setExplicitSet(null)
  }, [explicitSet, baselineSets.dataUpdatedAt])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (pinnedRunId > 0 && pinnedRunId !== selectedRunId) {
      setSelectedRunId(pinnedRunId)
    }
  }, [pinnedRunId, selectedRunId])

  useEffect(() => {
    const itemFromUrl = Number(searchParams.get("item") || 0)
    if (searchParams.get("mode") === "correction" && itemFromUrl > 0 && selectedRunId <= 0) {
      if (pinnedRunId > 0) setSelectedRunId(pinnedRunId)
    }
  }, [searchParams, pinnedRunId, selectedRunId])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeCategories.length) return
    if (!activeCategories.some((category) => category.category_key === selectedCategoryKey)) {
      setSelectedCategoryKey(
        activeCategories.find((category) => category.category_key === "space_image")?.category_key
        ?? activeCategories[0].category_key,
      )
    }
  }, [activeCategories, selectedCategoryKey])

  useEffect(() => {
    setAssetPage(0)
  }, [selectedCategoryKey, selectedPackageId])

  useEffect(() => {
    setRunPage(0)
  }, [selectedRunId])

  useEffect(() => {
    setRealExecutionConfirmed(false)
  }, [
    selectedCategoryKey,
    selectedSetId,
    workflowVersionId,
    executionMode,
    modelExecutionMode,
  ])

  useEffect(() => {
    const loadedSets = baselineSets.data?.items ?? null
    if (!loadedBaselineSetsMatchCategory(selectedCategoryKey, loadedSets)) return
    const nextSetId = baselineSetIdAfterListLoad(
      selectedSetId,
      loadedSets,
      pinnedRunId,
      awaitingListSetId,
    )
    if (nextSetId !== selectedSetId) {
      setSelectedSetId(nextSetId)
      setSelectedRunId(0)
    }
  }, [baselineSets.data?.items, selectedCategoryKey, selectedSetId, pinnedRunId, awaitingListSetId])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loadedBaselineSelectionIsCurrent(
      selectedSetId,
      selectedSet.data?.summary.id,
    )) return
    const nextRunId = baselineRunIdAfterSetLoad(
      selectedRunId,
      selectedSet.data?.runs ?? null,
      pinnedRunId,
    )
    if (nextRunId !== selectedRunId) setSelectedRunId(nextRunId)
  }, [pinnedRunId, selectedRunId, selectedSet.data?.runs, selectedSet.data?.summary.id, selectedSetId])

  useEffect(() => {
    const run = runDetail.data?.summary
    if (!run) return
    if (run.status === "running" || workflowRuntimePending(run)) {
      // 同一轮被重试后会再次完成（评审 R3）：看到它又在跑就清掉旧的完成记录
      for (const key of handledRunCompletions) {
        if (key.startsWith(`${run.id}:`)) handledRunCompletions.delete(key)
      }
      return
    }
    // 09-30：同一轮同一状态只做一次。以前每次这个 effect 重跑（纠偏模式切换条目、组件重挂载）都让
    // 基线集概览（每次 368 KB + 1.6 MB，后端要为分类下所有 run 重算状态计数）失效重拉，
    // 0.147 的运营页面把这两个接口打成每 5 秒一次。
    // 结果计数也进键：重试后仍是同一终态但结果变了，也要再刷新一次（评审 R3）
    const completionKey = `${run.id}:${run.status}:${run.completed}:${run.failed}:${run.valid_predictions}`
    if (handledRunCompletions.has(completionKey)) return
    handledRunCompletions.add(completionKey)

    void refreshCompletedBaselineEvidence(queryClient, run)
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-sets"] })
    if (selectedSetId) {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-set", selectedSetId] })
    }
  }, [
    queryClient,
    runDetail.data?.summary.id,
    runDetail.data?.summary.status,
    runDetail.data?.summary.workflow_runtime.pending,
    // 结果计数进依赖（复核 F2）：没看到重试中的 running 时，计数变化也要让 effect 重跑
    runDetail.data?.summary.completed,
    runDetail.data?.summary.failed,
    runDetail.data?.summary.valid_predictions,
    selectedSetId,
  ])

  const upload = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (files: File[]) => baselineRegressionApi.uploadAssets(
      files,
      undefined,
      selectedCategoryKey,
    ),
    onSuccess: async ({ items, package: uploadedPackage }) => {
      setSelectedPackageId(uploadedPackage.id)
      setUseWholePackage(truthKind !== "structured_fields")
      setSelectedAssetIds((current) => {
        const next = new Set(current)
        items.forEach((item) => next.add(item.id))
        return next
      })
      setSelectedAssetsById((current) => ({
        ...current,
        ...Object.fromEntries(items.map((item) => [item.id, item])),
      }))
      setExpectedByAsset((current) => ({
        ...current,
        ...Object.fromEntries(
          items
            .filter((item) => item.suggested_expected_level)
            .map((item) => [item.id, item.suggested_expected_level]),
        ),
      }))
      await Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-assets"] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-packages"] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["material-packages"] }),
      ])
      toast.success(`已上传并选中素材包“${uploadedPackage.name}”（${items.length} 张）`)
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!assets.data?.items.length) return
    setExpectedByAsset((current) => {
      const next = { ...current }
      for (const asset of assets.data.items) {
        if (next[asset.id] === undefined && asset.suggested_expected_level) {
          next[asset.id] = asset.suggested_expected_level
        }
      }
      return next
    })
  }, [assets.data?.items])

  const createSet = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => {
      const visibleAssets = assets.data?.items ?? []
      if (truthKind === "material_aggregate" || truthKind === "dimension_grade") {
        const packageContract = truthKind === "material_aggregate"
          ? MATERIAL_AGGREGATE_CONTRACT
          : DIMENSION_GRADE_CONTRACT
        if (!selectedPackageId) throw new Error("案例维度基准集必须选择一个案例样本包")
        return baselineRegressionApi.createSet({
          name: name.trim(),
          description: description.trim(),
          default_expected_level: null,
          category_key: selectedCategoryKey,
          source_package_id: selectedPackageId,
          package_truth_contract_key: packageContract,
          items: [],
        })
      }
      if (truthKind === "structured_fields") {
        if (designCaseContract) {
          const draft = designCaseDraft
          const workflowInput = draft ? structuredTruthWorkflowInput(draft) : undefined
          if (
            !draft
            || !designCaseRepresentativeId
            || !structuredTruthReady(draft, structuredContracts)
            || !workflowInput
          ) {
            throw new Error("案例输入、全部图片身份或终态字段真值尚未填写完整")
          }
          return baselineRegressionApi.createSet({
            name: name.trim(),
            description: description.trim(),
            default_expected_level: null,
            category_key: selectedCategoryKey,
            items: [{
              asset_id: designCaseRepresentativeId,
              truth_contract_key: designCaseContract.contract_key,
              expected_result: structuredTruthExpectedResult(
                draft,
                designCaseContract,
              ),
              workflow_input: workflowInput,
              source_package_id: selectedPackageId || undefined,
            }],
          })
        }
        const items = Array.from(selectedAssetIds).map((assetId) => {
          const draft = structuredTruthByAsset[assetId]
          const contract = structuredTruthContract(draft, structuredContracts)
          if (!draft || !contract || !structuredTruthReady(draft, structuredContracts)) {
            throw new Error(`素材 #${assetId} 还没有填写完整的字段路线与素材 ID`)
          }
          return {
            asset_id: assetId,
            truth_contract_key: contract.contract_key,
            expected_result: structuredTruthExpectedResult(draft, contract),
            source_package_id: selectedPackageId || undefined,
          }
        })
        return baselineRegressionApi.createSet({
          name: name.trim(),
          description: description.trim(),
          default_expected_level: null,
          category_key: selectedCategoryKey,
          items,
        })
      }
      const expectedLevelOverrides = Object.fromEntries(
        visibleAssets
          .filter((asset) => {
            const value = expectedByAsset[asset.id]
            return value && value !== (asset.suggested_expected_level ?? defaultLevel)
          })
          .map((asset) => [asset.id, expectedByAsset[asset.id]]),
      )
      return baselineRegressionApi.createSet({
        name: name.trim(),
        description: description.trim(),
        default_expected_level: defaultLevel,
        category_key: selectedCategoryKey,
        ...(useWholePackage && selectedPackageId
          ? {
              source_package_id: selectedPackageId,
              expected_level_overrides: expectedLevelOverrides,
              items: [],
            }
          : {
              items: Array.from(selectedAssetIds).map((assetId) => {
                const asset = visibleAssets.find((item) => item.id === assetId)
                return {
                  asset_id: assetId,
                  expected_level: expectedByAsset[assetId]
                    ?? asset?.suggested_expected_level
                    ?? defaultLevel,
                  source_package_id: selectedPackageId || undefined,
                }
              }),
            }),
      })
    },
    onSuccess: async (created) => {
      selectReturnedSet(created)
      setBaselineSetDialogOpen(false)
      setSelectedAssetIds(new Set())
      setExpectedByAsset({})
      setStructuredTruthByAsset({})
      setEditingStructuredAssetId(0)
      setUseWholePackage(false)
      setName("")
      setDescription("")
      await rereadSetListAfterCreate(created.category_key)
      toast.success(`基准集“${created.name}”已冻结`)
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const createBalanced100 = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.createBalanced100(),
    onSuccess: async ({ summary, idempotent, item_count }) => {
      const switched = selectReturnedSet(summary)
      await rereadSetListAfterCreate(summary.category_key)
      // 这个接口是幂等的：集合建过一次之后，再点只是切换选中。说清楚"已切换、
      // 什么都没新建"，否则界面看不出变化会被当成没生效。
      if (idempotent) {
        const frozenAt = formatPlatformDate(summary.created_at)
        toast.success(
          switched
            ? `已切换到均衡基准集（${item_count} 张，${frozenAt} 冻结）`
            : `均衡基准集已存在（${item_count} 张，${frozenAt} 冻结）`,
          { description: "这份样本已存在，本次没有新建。要纳入新标注素材请用「重建均衡样本」。" },
        )
      } else {
        toast.success(`均衡基准集已冻结（${item_count} 张）`)
      }
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const rebuildSurvey = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["baseline-sets", "balanced-rebuild-survey"],
    queryFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.surveyBalancedRebuild(),
    enabled: balancedRebuildDrawerOpen && selectedCategoryKey === "inspiration_image",
  })

  const rebuildBalanced = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.rebuildBalancedSample({
      per_level: rebuildPerLevel,
      strategy: rebuildStrategy,
      seed: rebuildSeed,
    }),
    onSuccess: async (result) => {
      const switched = selectReturnedSet(result.summary)
      setBalancedRebuildDrawerOpen(false)
      await rereadSetListAfterCreate(result.summary.category_key)
      if (result.idempotent) {
        toast.success(
          switched
            ? `这套参数的样本已存在，已切换（${result.item_count} 张）`
            : `这套参数的样本已存在（${result.item_count} 张）`,
          { description: "同一批素材配同一组参数抽出的是同一份清单。换一个随机种子才会得到新的抽样。" },
        )
      } else {
        toast.success(
          `新均衡样本已冻结（${result.item_count} 张）`,
          { description: `其中 ${result.coverage.new_asset_count} 张是原样本没有的素材；原样本与它的历史回归未被改动。` },
        )
      }
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const createRun = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => {
      const mechanismPayload = buildBaselineRunPayload({
        workflowVersionId,
        executionMode,
        categoryKey: selectedSet.data?.summary.category_key,
        modelExecutionMode,
        inferenceMode,
        realExecutionConfirmed,
      })
      return baselineRegressionApi.createRun(selectedSetId, {
        ...mechanismPayload,
      })
    },
    onSuccess: async (run) => {
      setRealExecutionConfirmed(false)
      selectRun(run.id, run.category_key)
      await Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-sets"] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-set", selectedSetId] }),
      ])
      toast.success(`第 ${run.sequence_no} 轮基准回归已启动`)
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  // 素材分页展示，但基准集常常要一次冻结整批（例如四批各 100 张的知识图谱样本）。
  // 逐页勾选既慢又容易漏，这里按同一筛选条件把每页的 id 取全再一次性选中。
  const selectAllAcrossPages = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      const selectedAssets: Asset[] = []
      for (const offset of selectionPageOffsets(
        assets.data?.total ?? 0,
        ASSET_PAGE_SIZE,
      )) {
        const page = await baselineRegressionApi.listAssets(
          selectedPackageId || undefined,
          selectedCategoryKey,
          offset,
          ASSET_PAGE_SIZE,
        )
        selectedAssets.push(...page.items)
        if (!page.items.length) break
      }
      return selectedAssets
    },
    onSuccess: (selectedAssets) => {
      const ids = selectedAssets.map((asset) => asset.id)
      setSelectedAssetsById((current) => ({
        ...current,
        ...Object.fromEntries(selectedAssets.map((asset) => [asset.id, asset])),
      }))
      setSelectedAssetIds((current) => addToSelection(current, ids))
      toast.success(`已选中 ${ids.length} 张素材`)
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const pageAssetIds = (assets.data?.items ?? []).map((asset) => asset.id)
  const allSelected = isPageFullySelected(selectedAssetIds, pageAssetIds)
  const selectedPackage = packages.data?.items.find(
    (item: MaterialPackage) => item.id === selectedPackageId,
  )
  const creationCount = useWholePackage
    ? selectedPackage?.active_asset_count ?? 0
    : selectedAssetIds.size
  const isCaseCategory = selectedCategoryKey === "design_case"
  const structuredReadyCount = designCaseContract
    ? (
        designCaseDraft
        && selectedDesignCaseAssets.length === selectedAssetIds.size
        && structuredTruthReady(designCaseDraft, structuredContracts)
          ? selectedAssetIds.size
          : 0
      )
    : Array.from(selectedAssetIds).filter((assetId) => (
        structuredTruthReady(structuredTruthByAsset[assetId], structuredContracts)
      )).length
  const creationTruthReady = (
    truthKind === "level"
    || truthKind === "material_aggregate"
    || truthKind === "dimension_grade"
    || structuredReadyCount === selectedAssetIds.size
  )
  const editingStructuredAsset = assets.data?.items.find(
    (item) => item.id === editingStructuredAssetId,
  )
  const assetPageCount = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil((assets.data?.total ?? 0) / ASSET_PAGE_SIZE),
  )
  const selectedRun = selectedSet.data?.runs.find((run) => run.id === selectedRunId)
  const summary = runDetail.data?.summary
  // 只取消当前这一轮。评测进度页的「取消全部」没有 run 作用域，
  // 会把当时所有回归一并判失败，运营需要的是这个按 run 的入口。
  const cancelRun = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.cancelRun(selectedRunId),
    onSuccess: async (result) => {
      await Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-sets"] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-set", selectedSetId] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-regression", selectedRunId] }),
      ])
      toast.success(`已取消本轮，共 ${result.affected} 个未完成任务`)
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })
  const workspaceLayout = baselineWorkspaceLayout(correctionItemId)

  useEffect(() => {
    const categoryKey = runDetail.data?.summary.category_key
    const baselineSetId = runDetail.data?.baseline_set.id
    if (baselineRunRouteSearch(searchParams, selectedRunId, runDetail.data?.summary)) return
    if (
      !loadedBaselineSelectionIsCurrent(selectedRunId, runDetail.data?.summary.id)
      || !categoryKey
      || !baselineSetId
    ) return
    const patch = baselineRunContextPatch(
      selectedCategoryKey,
      selectedSetId,
      { categoryKey, baselineSetId },
    )
    if (patch.categoryKey) {
      setSelectedCategoryKey(patch.categoryKey)
      return
    }
    if (patch.baselineSetId) setSelectedSetId(patch.baselineSetId)
  }, [
    runDetail.data?.baseline_set.id,
    runDetail.data?.summary.category_key,
    runDetail.data?.summary.id,
    selectedCategoryKey,
    selectedRunId,
    selectedSetId,
    searchParams,
  ])

  function toggleAsset(assetId: number) {
    setSelectedAssetIds((current) => {
      const next = new Set(current)
      if (next.has(assetId)) {
        next.delete(assetId)
        if (editingStructuredAssetId === assetId) setEditingStructuredAssetId(0)
      }
      else next.add(assetId)
      return next
    })
  }

  function selectTruthKind(next: TruthKind) {
    setTruthKind(next)
    // 两个案例维度都只能整包创建：真值是导入时按案例冻结的，逐条勾选没有意义
    setUseWholePackage(next === "material_aggregate" || next === "dimension_grade")
    setEditingStructuredAssetId(0)
    setSelectedAssetIds(new Set())
    if (next === "structured_fields") setExpectedByAsset({})
    else setStructuredTruthByAsset({})
  }

  // 换基准集的唯一入口：点列表、新建、切到均衡集、重建均衡样本都走这里。
  // 当前运行和地址栏里钉住的运行一起放下，否则页面会被那次运行拉回它自己的基准集。
  function selectSet(setId: number) {
    setLeftUrlRun(urlRunId > 0 ? { runId: urlRunId, locationKey: routeLocation.key } : null)
    // 直接问缓存而不是用渲染时的值：成功回调可能赶在列表的新结果渲染出来之前执行。
    setExplicitSet({
      id: setId,
      listUpdatedAt: queryClient.getQueryState(baselineSetsQueryKey)?.dataUpdatedAt ?? 0,
    })
    setSelectedSetId(setId)
    setSelectedRunId(0)
    setSearchParams(baselineRouteSearch(selectedCategoryKey), { replace: true })
  }

  // 新建、切到均衡集、重建均衡样本成功以后选中返回的基准集。
  // 等结果的这段时间里用户已经换到别的类目，就不再把页面带回去。返回是否真的切过去了。
  function selectReturnedSet(summary: { id: number; category_key: string }): boolean {
    if (summary.category_key !== selectedCategoryKey) return false
    selectSet(summary.id)
    return true
  }

  // 重新读它所在类目的列表。创建完成之前发出、还没回来的列表请求里没有这个基准集，先作废，
  // 免得那份旧结果被当成“重新读到的列表”，把刚选中的基准集换掉。
  async function rereadSetListAfterCreate(categoryKey: string) {
    await queryClient.cancelQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-sets", categoryKey, "overview"] })
    await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-sets"] })
  }

  function selectRun(runId: number, categoryKey: string) {
    setLeftUrlRun(null)
    setExplicitSet(null)
    setSearchParams(baselineSelectedRunSearch(categoryKey, runId))
    setSelectedRunId(runId)
  }

  function selectCategory(categoryKey: string) {
    if (categoryKey === selectedCategoryKey) return
    setSelectedCategoryKey(categoryKey)
    setSelectedPackageId(0)
    setUseWholePackage(false)
    setSelectedAssetIds(new Set())
    setSelectedAssetsById({})
    setExpectedByAsset({})
    setTruthKind("level")
    setStructuredTruthByAsset({})
    setEditingStructuredAssetId(0)
    setLeftUrlRun(urlRunId > 0 ? { runId: urlRunId, locationKey: routeLocation.key } : null)
    setExplicitSet(null)
    setSelectedSetId(0)
    setSelectedRunId(0)
    setSearchParams(baselineRouteSearch(categoryKey), { replace: true })
  }

  function openCorrection(itemId: number) {
    if (!selectedRunId) return
    setSearchParams(baselineRouteSearch(selectedCategoryKey, {
      runId: selectedRunId,
      itemId,
    }))
  }

  function closeCorrection() {
    setSearchParams(baselineRouteSearch(selectedCategoryKey), { replace: true })
  }

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        index="03.7"
        title="存量回归与纠偏"
        description="导入一批素材，选择任一完整评测机制反复试跑，并逐条补充人工真值。每轮都会固定使用当时选择的提示词、规则模块和模型参数，互不覆盖。"
        actions={
          <>
              <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => { window.location.href = `/workflow/optimization/image-generation?set=${selectedSetId}` }} disabled={!selectedSetId}>AI 生图回归</Button>
            <input
              ref={uploadRef}
              className="hidden"
              type="file"
              accept={(selectedCategory?.allowed_mime_types ?? [
                "image/jpeg",
                "image/png",
                "image/webp",
                "image/gif",
              ]).join(",")}
              multiple
              onChange={(event) => {
                const files = event.target.files
                  ? Array.from(event.target.files)
                  : []
                event.target.value = ""
                if (files.length) upload.mutate(files)
              }}
            />
            <Button
              variant="secondary"
              onClick={() => uploadRef.current?.click()}
              disabled={!access.user?.is_admin || upload.isPending}
            >
              <CloudArrowUp />{upload.isPending ? "正在上传" : "上传基准素材"}
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="secondary"
              onClick={() => setRuleDiagnosticsDrawerOpen(true)}
              disabled={!selectedRunId}
            >
              规则命中诊断
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="secondary"
              onClick={() => setSemanticQualityDrawerOpen(true)}
              disabled={!selectedRunId}
            >
              语义字段质量
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="secondary"
              onClick={() => {
                assets.refetch()
                packages.refetch()
                baselineSets.refetch()
                if (selectedSetId) selectedSet.refetch()
                if (selectedRunId) {
                  runDetail.refetch()
                  fieldMetrics.refetch()
                  semanticMetrics.refetch()
                }
              }}
            >
              <ArrowClockwise />刷新
            </Button>
          </>
        }
      />

      <section className="shell-content-wide baseline-context">
        <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(260px,420px)_minmax(0,1fr)] lg:items-end">
          <label>
            <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-bold">评测类目</span>
            <select
              className="visual-native-select w-full font-semibold focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary"
              value={selectedCategoryKey}
              onChange={(event) => selectCategory(event.target.value)}
            >
              {activeCategories.map((category) => (
                <option key={category.category_key} value={category.category_key}>
                  {category.display_name} · {category.category_key}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
          <div className="border-l-2 border-primary pl-4">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <strong className="text-sm">当前流水线：{selectedCategory?.display_name ?? selectedCategoryKey}</strong>
              <Badge tone="active">类目隔离</Badge>
              <Badge>
                {selectedCategory?.pipeline_config.dimensions.enabled === false
                  ? "当前仅提示词"
                  : `当前维度 ${selectedCategory?.dimension_schema_version ?? "未绑定"}`}
              </Badge>
            </div>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
              {selectedCategory?.description || "素材包、冻结基准集、回归运行和纠偏分析均只在当前类目内流转。"}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="shell-content-wide baseline-selection" aria-label="选择基准集">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
          <label className="min-w-0 flex-1 basis-80">
            <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-bold">选择基准集</span>
            <select
              aria-label="选择基准集"
              data-testid="baseline-set-selector"
              className="visual-native-select w-full"
              value={baselineSets.data?.items.some(item => item.id === selectedSetId) ? selectedSetId : ""}
              disabled={baselineSets.isLoading || !baselineSets.data?.items.length}
              onChange={event => selectSet(Number(event.target.value))}
            >
              <option value="" disabled>选择基准集</option>
              {baselineSets.data?.items.map(item => (
                <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>
                  #{item.id} {item.name} · {item.item_count} 张 · {item.truth_kind === "structured_fields" ? "结构化字段" : `默认 ${item.default_expected_level}`} · {item.run_count} 轮
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
          <Button variant="secondary" disabled={!access.user?.is_admin} onClick={() => setBaselineSetDialogOpen(true)}>
            创建冻结基准集
          </Button>
            {/* 这个接口幂等：首次点会冻结，之后只切换。文案说"切换"而不是"生成"，
                因为叫"生成"会让人以为每次都重抽一份，看不到变化就以为坏了。 */}
            {selectedCategoryKey === "inspiration_image" && <Button
              variant="secondary"
              title="L1-L5 各 20 张的既有均衡样本；已冻结，点击只切换到它，不会重新抽样"
              onClick={() => createBalanced100.mutate()}
              disabled={!access.user?.is_admin || createBalanced100.isPending}
            >
              <CheckSquare />{createBalanced100.isPending ? "正在校验" : "切换到均衡基准集"}
            </Button>}
            {selectedCategoryKey === "inspiration_image" && <Button
              variant="secondary"
              title="用当前全部人工评级素材重新抽样，冻结为新的基准集；原样本与其历史回归不受影响"
              onClick={() => setBalancedRebuildDrawerOpen(true)}
            >
              <ArrowsClockwise />重建均衡样本
            </Button>}
        </div>
        {!baselineSets.isLoading && !baselineSets.data?.items.length && (
          <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            尚无基准集。先上传或选择已有素材后创建。
          </p>
        )}
      </section>

      <div className={`shell-content-wide baseline-workspace-body ${correctionItemId > 0 ? workspaceLayout.gridClassName : ""}`}>


        <main className={`min-w-0 px-5 py-7 md:px-8 lg:py-9 ${correctionItemId > 0 ? "lg:px-6 xl:px-8" : "lg:px-10"}`}>
          <BaselineSetDialog open={baselineSetDialogOpen} onOpenChange={setBaselineSetDialogOpen}>
          <section className="visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
            <div className="border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4">
              <h2 className="visual-card-title">创建冻结基准集</h2>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                {truthKind === "dimension_grade"
                  ? "选择一个已导入的案例两维度样本包，真值是案例档位加逐图人工档位；模型分按工作流版本里冻结的档位阈值投影后逐图比对。"
                  : truthKind === "material_aggregate"
                  ? "选择一个已导入的案例样本包，真值直接用导入时冻结的图片总数、分数分布与类型分布；只有带图片统计的案例会进集。"
                  : truthKind === "level"
                  ? "上传会复用现有素材去重规则。整批等级作为默认真值，也可在入集前逐张覆盖；创建后不可修改。"
                  : designCaseContract
                    ? "选择组成同一案例的全部图片，填写案例运行输入与终态字段真值；案例与图片身份会一起冻结。"
                    : "逐张选择知识图谱字段路线并填写人工真值；路线、素材标识和字段内容会一起冻结，供工作流反复回归与纠偏。"}
              </p>
            </div>
            {structuredContracts.length > 0 && (
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-5 py-4">
                <span className="mr-2 text-xs font-bold">真值类型</span>
                <Button
                  variant={truthKind === "level" ? "primary" : "secondary"}
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={() => selectTruthKind("level")}
                >
                  等级真值（L1–L5）
                </Button>
                <Button
                  variant={truthKind === "structured_fields" ? "primary" : "secondary"}
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={() => selectTruthKind("structured_fields")}
                >
                  {designCaseContract ? "字段真值（案例）" : "字段真值（知识图谱）"}
                </Button>
                {isCaseCategory ? (
                  <>
                    <Button
                      variant={truthKind === "material_aggregate" ? "primary" : "secondary"}
                      size="sm"
                      onClick={() => selectTruthKind("material_aggregate")}
                    >
                      素材维度（案例聚合）
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      variant={truthKind === "dimension_grade" ? "primary" : "secondary"}
                      size="sm"
                      onClick={() => selectTruthKind("dimension_grade")}
                    >
                      图片维度（逐图档位）
                    </Button>
                  </>
                ) : null}
                <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                  两种真值分别冻结，不能在同一基准集中混用。
                </span>
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="grid gap-4 border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-5 py-4 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(280px,1fr)_auto] lg:items-end">
              <label>
                <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">从素材包选择（推荐）</span>
                <select
                  className="visual-native-select w-full"
                  value={selectedPackageId || ""}
                  onChange={(event) => {
                    const packageId = event.target.value ? Number(event.target.value) : 0
                    setSelectedPackageId(packageId)
                    setUseWholePackage(truthKind !== "structured_fields" && Boolean(packageId))
                    setSelectedAssetIds(new Set())
                    setExpectedByAsset({})
                    setStructuredTruthByAsset({})
                  }}
                >
                  <option value="">{isCasePackageTruth ? "请选择已导入的案例样本包" : "不限定素材包，逐张选择"}</option>
                  {(packages.data?.items ?? []).map((item) => (
                    <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>
                      {item.name} · {item.active_asset_count} 张可用
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </label>
              {truthKind === "level" ? (
                <label className="visual-native-choice rounded-md flex h-11 cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-3 text-sm font-semibold">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    className="visual-native-checkbox size-4"
                    checked={useWholePackage}
                    disabled={!selectedPackageId}
                    onChange={(event) => {
                      setUseWholePackage(event.target.checked)
                      setSelectedAssetIds(new Set())
                      setExpectedByAsset({})
                    }}
                  />
                  整包加入，无需逐张勾选
                </label>
              ) : truthKind === "material_aggregate" || truthKind === "dimension_grade" ? (
                <div className="flex h-11 items-center border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-044 px-3 text-sm font-semibold rounded-md">
                  整包加入，真值随案例样本一起冻结
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="flex h-11 items-center border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-044 px-3 text-sm font-semibold rounded-md">
                  {designCaseContract ? "选择组成同一案例的全部图片" : "逐张选择字段路线"}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="grid gap-4 px-5 py-5 min-[1280px]:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,1fr)] min-[1280px]:items-end min-[1750px]:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,160px)_auto]">
              <label>
                <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">基准集名称</span>
                <Input
                  value={name}
                  maxLength={160}
                  placeholder={isCasePackageTruth ? "例如：案例图片维度回归基准" : "例如：2026-07 全 L1 基准"}
                  onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}
                />
              </label>
              <label>
                <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">说明（可选）</span>
                <Input
                  value={description}
                  maxLength={2000}
                  placeholder="记录来源与确认口径"
                  onChange={(event) => setDescription(event.target.value)}
                />
              </label>
              {truthKind === "level" ? (
                <LevelSelect
                  label="整批期望等级"
                  value={defaultLevel}
                  onChange={(value) => setDefaultLevel(value)}
                />
              ) : isCasePackageTruth ? (
                <div className="border-l-2 border-primary pl-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                  真值来自案例样本导入，无需在此填写
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="border-l-2 border-primary pl-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                  已填写 <strong className="text-[var(--ink)]">{structuredReadyCount}</strong> / {selectedAssetIds.size} 张
                </div>
              )}
              <Button
                disabled={!access.user?.is_admin || !name.trim() || !creationCount || !creationTruthReady || createSet.isPending}
                onClick={() => createSet.mutate()}
              >
                {createSet.isPending
                  ? "正在冻结"
                  : designCaseContract && truthKind === "structured_fields"
                    ? `创建基准集 (1 案例 / ${creationCount} 图片)`
                    : `创建基准集 (${creationCount})`}
              </Button>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-5 py-3">
              <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                {isCasePackageTruth ? (
                  selectedPackage
                    ? <>将整包加入：<strong className="text-foreground">{selectedPackage.name}</strong> · {creationCount} 个可用案例，真值随导入记录冻结</>
                    : <>请选择已导入的案例样本包</>
                ) : useWholePackage ? (
                  <>将整包加入：<strong className="text-foreground">{selectedPackage?.name}</strong> · {creationCount} 张可用</>
                ) : (
                  <>已选择 <strong className="text-foreground">{selectedAssetIds.size}</strong> / {assets.data?.total ?? 0} 张</>
                )}
              </p>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <Button
                  variant="ghost"
                  size="sm"
                  disabled={useWholePackage || !assets.data?.items.length}
                  onClick={() => {
                    setSelectedAssetIds((current) => (
                      togglePageSelection(current, pageAssetIds)
                    ))
                  }}
                >
                  {allSelected ? <CheckSquare weight="fill" /> : <Square />}
                  {allSelected ? "取消本页全选" : "全选本页素材"}
                </Button>
                <Button
                  variant="ghost"
                  size="sm"
                  disabled={
                    useWholePackage
                    || !assets.data?.total
                    || selectAllAcrossPages.isPending
                  }
                  onClick={() => selectAllAcrossPages.mutate()}
                >
                  <Square />
                  {selectAllAcrossPages.isPending
                    ? "正在选取全部…"
                    : `全选全部 ${assets.data?.total ?? 0} 张`}
                </Button>
                {truthKind === "level" && <Button
                  variant="secondary"
                  size="sm"
                  disabled={
                    useWholePackage
                      ? !assets.data?.items.length
                      : !selectedAssetIds.size
                  }
                  onClick={() => {
                    const next: Record<number, BaselineLevel> = {}
                    const assetIds = useWholePackage
                      ? assets.data?.items.map((asset) => asset.id) ?? []
                      : Array.from(selectedAssetIds)
                    assetIds.forEach((assetId) => { next[assetId] = defaultLevel })
                    setExpectedByAsset((current) => ({ ...current, ...next }))
                  }}
                >
                  当前{useWholePackage ? "预览" : "所选"}全部改为 {defaultLevel}
                </Button>}
              </div>
            </div>
            {useWholePackage && truthKind === "level" && (
              <div className="border-t border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-037 px-5 py-3 text-xs font-semibold">
                已启用整包模式：单个基准集最多 10000 张。系统先按文件名中的 L1–L5 / 好 / 中等 / 中差 / 极差 / 过滤预填；下方按页预览并可逐张修改，未访问页面的素材同样由服务端解析。
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
              <span>第 {assetPage + 1} / {assetPageCount} 页 · 每页最多 {ASSET_PAGE_SIZE} 张</span>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <Button
                  variant="ghost"
                  size="sm"
                  disabled={assetPage <= 0 || assets.isFetching}
                  onClick={() => setAssetPage((current) => Math.max(0, current - 1))}
                >
                  上一页
                </Button>
                <Button
                  variant="ghost"
                  size="sm"
                  disabled={assetPage + 1 >= assetPageCount || assets.isFetching}
                  onClick={() => setAssetPage((current) => current + 1)}
                >
                  下一页
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="max-h-[440px] overflow-auto">
              {assets.isLoading ? (
                <div className="h-48 animate-pulse bg-white" />
              ) : assets.data?.items.length ? (
                <table className={`w-full border-collapse text-left text-sm ${truthKind === "structured_fields" ? "min-w-[900px]" : "min-w-[720px]"}`}>
                  <thead className="sticky top-0 z-10 bg-white">
                    <tr className="border-b border-[var(--line)] text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                      <th className="w-14 px-5 py-3"><span className="sr-only">选择</span></th>
                      <th className="px-3 py-3">素材</th>
                      <th className="px-3 py-3">尺寸</th>
                      <th className="w-64 px-5 py-3">
                        {isCasePackageTruth ? "导入真值" : truthKind === "structured_fields" ? "字段路线与真值" : "期望等级"}
                      </th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {assets.data.items.map((asset) => {
                      const checked = useWholePackage || selectedAssetIds.has(asset.id)
                      const suggestedLevel = asset.suggested_expected_level
                      const effectiveLevel = expectedByAsset[asset.id]
                        ?? suggestedLevel
                        ?? defaultLevel
                      const manuallyChanged = expectedByAsset[asset.id] !== undefined
                        && expectedByAsset[asset.id] !== (suggestedLevel ?? defaultLevel)
                      const truthDraft = designCaseContract
                        ? designCaseDraft ?? createStructuredTruthDraft(designCaseContract)
                        : structuredTruthByAsset[asset.id] ?? createStructuredTruthDraft()
                      const selectedTruthContract = structuredTruthContract(
                        truthDraft,
                        structuredContracts,
                      )
                      const truthReady = structuredTruthReady(
                        truthDraft,
                        structuredContracts,
                      )
                      const filenameTruthSuggestion = asset.knowledge_graph_truth_suggestion
                      const filenamePrefilled = truthDraft.source === "filename"
                      return (
                        <tr
                          key={asset.id}
                          className={`border-b border-[var(--line)] last:border-0 ${checked ? "bg-alias-surface-044" : "hover:bg-alias-surface-056"}`}
                        >
                          <td className="px-5 py-3">
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              className="visual-native-button p-0 flex items-center justify-center focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary"
                              aria-label={`${checked ? "取消选择" : "选择"}${asset.name}`}
                              disabled={useWholePackage}
                              onClick={() => toggleAsset(asset.id)}
                            >
                              {checked ? <CheckSquare size={20} weight="fill" /> : <Square size={20} />}
                            </button>
                          </td>
                          <td className="px-3 py-3">
                            <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
                              {!isCasePackageTruth && asset.mime_type.startsWith("image/") && (
                                <ImagePreviewButton
                                  src={asset.image_url}
                                  alt={asset.name}
                                  imageClassName="size-12"
                                  onPreview={setImagePreview}
                                />
                              )}
                              <div className="min-w-0">
                                <p className="file-name max-w-lg truncate text-sm">{asset.name}</p>
                                <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                                  <p className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{isCasePackageTruth ? "案例" : "素材"} <span className="visual-code">#{asset.id}</span></p>
                                  {isCasePackageTruth ? (
                                    <Badge tone="neutral">导入冻结真值</Badge>
                                  ) : truthKind === "structured_fields" ? (
                                    <Badge tone={
                                      filenameTruthSuggestion?.status === "conflict" && checked
                                        ? "warning"
                                        : truthReady
                                          ? "success"
                                          : checked
                                            ? "active"
                                            : "neutral"
                                    }>
                                      {/* 就绪与否优先于来源：文件名识别出来且已就绪的，
                                          不该再显示「待确认」制造并不存在的工作量。 */}
                                      {truthReady
                                        ? filenamePrefilled
                                          ? "名称已识别"
                                          : "字段已填写"
                                        : filenameTruthSuggestion?.status === "conflict" && checked
                                          ? "名称冲突"
                                          : filenamePrefilled
                                            ? "名称已识别，素材 ID 待填"
                                            : checked
                                              ? "需手动填写"
                                              : "待勾选"}
                                    </Badge>
                                  ) : (
                                    <Badge tone={manuallyChanged ? "active" : suggestedLevel ? "success" : "neutral"}>
                                      {manuallyChanged
                                        ? "已手动调整"
                                        : suggestedLevel
                                          ? `文件名预填 ${suggestedLevel}`
                                          : `整批默认 ${defaultLevel}`}
                                    </Badge>
                                  )}
                                </div>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          </td>
                          <td className="font-data px-3 py-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                            {asset.width && asset.height ? `${asset.width} × ${asset.height}` : "—"}
                          </td>
                          <td className="px-5 py-3">
                            {isCasePackageTruth ? (
                              <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">使用导入时冻结的人工真值；创建后可在真值管理中查看与纠偏。</p>
                            ) : truthKind === "structured_fields" ? (
                              <div>
                                <Button
                                  variant="secondary"
                                  size="sm"
                                  className="w-full"
                                  disabled={!checked}
                                  onClick={() => {
                                    setStructuredTruthByAsset((current) => {
                                      if (designCaseContract && designCaseRepresentativeId) {
                                        const previous = current[designCaseRepresentativeId]
                                          ?? createStructuredTruthDraft(designCaseContract)
                                        return {
                                          [designCaseRepresentativeId]: syncDesignCaseWorkflowInput(
                                            previous,
                                            selectedDesignCaseAssets,
                                          ),
                                        }
                                      }
                                      return current[asset.id]
                                        ? current
                                        : { ...current, [asset.id]: createStructuredTruthDraft() }
                                    })
                                    setEditingStructuredAssetId(asset.id)
                                  }}
                                >
                                  {truthDraft.contractKey
                                    ? "查看 / 修改字段真值"
                                    : "填写字段真值"}
                                </Button>
                                <p className="mt-2 truncate text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                                  {selectedTruthContract
                                    ? `${selectedTruthContract.display_name} · ${truthReady ? selectedTruthContract.identity_label + " 已填" : designCaseContract ? "案例输入待填" : "素材 ID 待填"}`
                                    : filenameTruthSuggestion?.status === "conflict"
                                      ? filenameTruthSuggestion.reason ?? "名称路线与素材 ID 类型冲突"
                                      : checked
                                        ? "请选择国内/海外与整体/单体"
                                        : "勾选后按文件名自动识别路线"}
                                </p>
                              </div>
                            ) : (
                              <select
                                aria-label={`${asset.name}期望等级`}
                                className="visual-native-select w-full disabled:bg-alias-surface-014 disabled:text-[var(--muted)]"
                                disabled={!checked}
                                value={effectiveLevel}
                                onChange={(event) => setExpectedByAsset((current) => ({
                                  ...current,
                                  [asset.id]: event.target.value as BaselineLevel,
                                }))}
                              >
                                {levels.map((level) => <option key={level} value={level}>{level}</option>)}
                              </select>
                            )}
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      )
                    })}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              ) : (
                <p className="px-5 py-12 text-center text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">
                  还没有可选素材，请先上传基准图片。
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          </section>
          </BaselineSetDialog>
          <StructuredTruthDrawer
            open={editingStructuredAssetId > 0}
            onOpenChange={(open) => {
              if (!open) setEditingStructuredAssetId(0)
            }}
            assetName={editingStructuredAsset?.name ?? `素材 #${editingStructuredAssetId}`}
            draft={designCaseContract
              ? designCaseDraft ?? createStructuredTruthDraft(designCaseContract)
              : structuredTruthByAsset[editingStructuredAssetId] ?? createStructuredTruthDraft()}
            contracts={structuredContracts}
            selectedAssets={designCaseContract ? selectedDesignCaseAssets : (editingStructuredAsset ? [editingStructuredAsset] : [])}
            onChange={(draft) => setStructuredTruthByAsset((current) => ({
              ...current,
              [designCaseContract ? designCaseRepresentativeId : editingStructuredAssetId]: {
                ...draft,
                source: "manual",
              },
            }))}
          />

          {selectedSet.data ? (
            <div className="space-y-6">
              <Card aria-label="回归进度" data-testid="baseline-progress">
              <h2 className="visual-card-title mb-5">回归进度</h2>
              <div className="border-b border-[var(--line)] pb-5">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4">
                <div>
                  <p className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                    基准集 #<span className="visual-code">{selectedSet.data.summary.id}</span> · {selectedSet.data.summary.item_count} 张 · 指纹 {selectedSet.data.summary.fingerprint.slice(0, 12)}
                  </p>
                  <h2 className="visual-item-title mt-2" data-testid="baseline-set-name">{selectedSet.data.summary.name}</h2>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                    {selectedSet.data.summary.truth_kind === "structured_fields" ? "结构化字段" : `默认 ${selectedSet.data.summary.default_expected_level}`} · {selectedSet.data.summary.run_count} 轮 · {selectedSet.data.summary.created_by} · {formatPlatformDate(selectedSet.data.summary.created_at)}
                  </p>
              {selectedSet.data.summary.description && (
                    <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">{selectedSet.data.summary.description}</p>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => setRunConfigDrawerOpen(true)}>调整运行配置</Button>
                  {selectedRunId > 0 && <BaselineExportButton runId={selectedRunId} />}
                  {selectedRunId > 0 && <StockExportPanel key={selectedRunId} source="baseline" runId={String(selectedRunId)} />}
                  {selectedRunId > 0 && (
                    <BaselineTruthImportButton
                      runId={selectedRunId}
                      onApplied={async () => { await runDetail.refetch() }}
                    />
                  )}
                  <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => setRunHistoryDrawerOpen(true)}>
                    查看运行历史
                  </Button>
                </div>

                </div>
                <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]" data-testid="truth-maintenance-link">
                  人工真值在质量资产里看图维护，不经回归纠偏：
                  <Link className="ml-1 font-semibold underline" to={`/workflow/quality-assets?set=${selectedSet.data.summary.id}&category_key=${encodeURIComponent(selectedSet.data.summary.category_key)}`}>去维护基准集真值</Link>
                </p>
                <div className="mt-4">
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="text-xs font-semibold">运行配置</p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                      {selectedWorkflowVersion ? `${selectedWorkflowVersion.workflow_name} · V${selectedWorkflowVersion.version}` : "尚未选择完整评测机制"}
                      {" · "}{executionMode === "structured" ? "标准评分合同" : "自由实验"}
                      {" · "}{modelExecutionSummary(modelExecutionMode).label}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                </div>
                <RunConfigDrawer
                  open={runConfigDrawerOpen}
                  onOpenChange={setRunConfigDrawerOpen}
                  footer={(
                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                      <div className="min-w-0 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                        <p className="font-semibold text-[var(--ink)]">启动摘要</p>
                        <p className="truncate">
                          {selectedWorkflowVersion
                            ? `${selectedWorkflowVersion.workflow_name} · 版本 ${selectedWorkflowVersion.version} · ${selectedWorkflowVersion.manifest.nodes?.length ?? 0} 个节点`
                            : "请先选择本轮完整评测机制"}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                      <Button
                        className="shrink-0"
                        disabled={
                          !access.can("regressions:run", selectedCategoryKey) || createRun.isPending
                          || stockWorkflowVersions.isLoading
                          || stockWorkflowVersions.isError
                          || !workflowVersionId
                          || Boolean(selectedWorkflowIssue)
                          || !realExecutionCanStart(modelExecutionMode, realExecutionConfirmed)
                          || (modelExecutionMode === "real" && !promptExecutionSummaries.length)
                          || !realEstimateReady
                          || realRequestCapExceeded
                          || selectedSet.data.runs.some((run) => run.status === "running")
                        }
                        onClick={() => createRun.mutate()}
                      >
                        <Play weight="fill" />
                        {createRun.isPending ? "正在启动" : "运行全量回归"}
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                >
                  <div className="space-y-7">
                    {summary && <RunSelectionSummary run={summary} />}
                    <section className="space-y-4" aria-labelledby="baseline-workflow-config">
                      <div>
                        <p id="baseline-workflow-config" className="text-sm font-bold">本轮完整评测机制</p>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">每次回归由运营自由选择一个完整版本。提示词、模型参数和规则模块会作为一个整体冻结，之后修改工作副本不会改变本轮结果。</p>
                      </div>
                      <label>
                        <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">选择评测机制版本</span>
                        <select
                          className="visual-native-select w-full"
                          value={workflowVersionId}
                          onChange={(event) => setWorkflowVersionId(Number(event.target.value))}
                        >
                          <option value={0}>请选择本轮要验证的完整版本</option>
                          {(stockWorkflowVersions.data ?? []).map((item) => {
                            const issue = mechanismVersionStockIssue(item)
                            return (
                              <option
                                key={item.id}
                                value={item.id}
                                disabled={Boolean(mechanismVersionStockIssue(item))}
                              >
                                {item.workflow_name} · 版本 {item.version}{issue ? `（不可运行：${issue}）` : ""}
                              </option>
                            )
                          })}
                        </select>
                      </label>
                      {stockWorkflowVersions.isError && <p className="text-xs text-alias-content-041">评测机制版本加载失败，暂时不能启动本轮回归，请重试。</p>}
                      {!stockWorkflowVersions.isLoading && !(stockWorkflowVersions.data ?? []).length && <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">当前类目还没有可用于存量回归的完整评测机制。请先在工作流管理器完成编排并生成版本。</p>}
                      {selectedWorkflowIssue && (
                        <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-041">
                          当前版本不能由原生存量执行器准确运行：{selectedWorkflowIssue}。请回到工作流管理器检查节点后再启动本轮回归。
                        </p>
                      )}
                      <a className="inline-flex text-xs font-semibold underline" href={`/workflow/governance/workflow-manager?category_key=${encodeURIComponent(selectedCategoryKey)}`}>去工作流管理器</a>
                      {selectedWorkflowVersion && (
                        <div className="space-y-2 border border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 p-3 rounded-md">
                          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
                            <span className="font-semibold">该版本包含的步骤</span>
                            <span className="visual-code text-[var(--muted)]">Hash {selectedWorkflowVersion.canonical_hash.slice(0, 12)}</span>
                          </div>
                          <div className="grid gap-2 md:grid-cols-2">
                            {(selectedWorkflowVersion.manifest.nodes ?? []).map((node, index) => (
                              <div key={node.node_instance_id} className="border border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-3 py-3 text-xs leading-5 rounded-md">
                                <p className="font-semibold">{index + 1}. {node.display_name}</p>
                                <p className="text-[var(--muted)]">{workflowNodeSummary(node)}</p>
                              </div>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </section>

                    <section className="space-y-4 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-6" aria-labelledby="baseline-execution-config">
                      <div>
                        <p id="baseline-execution-config" className="text-sm font-bold">执行方式</p>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">运行启动后会冻结完整评测机制和执行模式；历史记录不会被后续编辑覆盖。</p>
                      </div>
                      <label>
                        <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">结果判定方式</span>
                        <select
                          className="visual-native-select w-full"
                          value={executionMode}
                          onChange={(event) => setExecutionMode(event.target.value as "freeform" | "structured")}
                        >
                          <option value="freeform">自由实验（默认）</option>
                          <option value="structured">标准评分合同</option>
                        </select>
                      </label>
                      <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                        {baselineExecutionModeHelp(selectedSet.data.summary.truth_kind, executionMode)}
                      </p>
                    </section>

                    <section className="space-y-4 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-6" aria-labelledby="baseline-model-execution-config">
                      <div>
                        <p id="baseline-model-execution-config" className="text-sm font-bold">模型怎么运行</p>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">建议先用快速模拟检查流程；需要验证提示词实际效果时，再明确选择真实模型。</p>
                      </div>
                      <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
                        {(["simulated", "real"] as const).map((mode) => {
                          const summary = modelExecutionSummary(mode)
                          const selected = modelExecutionMode === mode
                          return (
                            <label
                              key={mode}
                              className={"visual-native-choice " + (`cursor-pointer border rounded-md px-4 py-4 ${selected ? "border-alias-edge-011 bg-alias-surface-044" : "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white"}`)}
                            >
                              <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold">
                                <input className="visual-native-radio"
                                  type="radio"
                                  name="model-execution-mode"
                                  value={mode}
                                  checked={selected}
                                  onChange={() => setModelExecutionMode(mode)}
                                />
                                {summary.label}
                              </span>
                              <span className="mt-2 block text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{summary.description}</span>
                            </label>
                          )
                        })}
                      </div>
                      <InferenceModeField
                        useOnlineInference={useOnlineInference}
                        onChange={(next) => { setUseOnlineInference(next); setRealExecutionConfirmed(false) }}
                      />
                      {modelExecutionMode === "real" && (
                        <div className="space-y-4 border border-alias-edge-049 bg-alias-surface-077 px-4 py-4 rounded-md">
                          <div>
                            <p className="text-sm font-bold text-alias-content-033">真实模型调用前请核对</p>
                            <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033">
                              素材将发送至火山方舟，并按实际用量产生费用。
                            </p>
                          </div>
                          {realExecutionPreview.isPending && (
                            <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033" data-testid="estimate-pending">正在按当前推理方式、机制与基准集估算用量…</p>
                          )}
                          {realExecutionPreview.errorMessage && (
                            <p className="border-l-2 border-danger pl-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-041" data-testid="estimate-error">
                              用量预估失败：{realExecutionPreview.errorMessage}。请先确认所选机制的模型已配置计价，以及本轮推理方式对应的接入点已绑定。
                            </p>
                          )}
                          {realBudget && <RunPricingEstimate preview={realBudget} />}
                          {realRequestCapExceeded && realBudget && (
                            <p className="border-l-2 border-danger pl-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-041">
                              本轮预估的{exceededCapLabels(realBudget.exceeded)}超过服务端硬上限，无法启动。请改用更小的基准集，或联系管理员调整上限。
                            </p>
                          )}
                          <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)] visual-panel border border-[var(--line)] bg-white">
                            {promptExecutionSummaries.map((prompt) => (
                              <div key={prompt.nodeInstanceId} className="grid gap-2 px-3 py-3 text-xs md:grid-cols-[minmax(150px,0.8fr)_minmax(0,1.2fr)]">
                                <div>
                                  <p className="font-bold">{prompt.displayName}</p>
                                  <p className="font-data mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">提示词版本 {prompt.promptVersion}</p>
                                </div>
                                <p className="leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                                  模型 {prompt.modelName}（<span className="visual-code">{prompt.modelId}</span>） · 最大 token {prompt.maxTokens?.toLocaleString("zh-CN") ?? "未标明"} · 思考层级 {thinkingLevelName(prompt.thinkingLevel)}
                                </p>
                              </div>
                            ))}
                            {!promptExecutionSummaries.length && (
                              <p className="px-3 py-4 text-xs text-alias-content-041">当前机制没有可调用的提示词节点，不能启动真实模型评测。</p>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <label className="visual-native-choice flex items-start gap-3 border-t border-alias-edge-049 pt-4 text-xs font-semibold leading-5 text-alias-content-018">
                            <input
                              type="checkbox"
                              className="visual-native-checkbox mt-0.5 size-4"
                              checked={realExecutionConfirmed}
                              onChange={(event) => setRealExecutionConfirmed(event.target.checked)}
                            />
                            <span>我确认使用真实模型并产生费用，也知晓本轮素材将发送至火山方舟。</span>
                          </label>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </section>
                  </div>
                </RunConfigDrawer>
              </div>


              <div className="my-5 grid items-stretch gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4" data-testid="baseline-progress-cells">
                <div className="min-w-0 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-3">
                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold">当前轮次</p>
                  <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <p className="font-data text-lg font-bold">{selectedRun ? `第 ${selectedRun.sequence_no} 轮` : "尚未运行"}</p>
                    {selectedRun && <Badge tone={statusTone(selectedRun)}>{statusName(selectedRun)}</Badge>}
                  </div>
                  {summary?.status === "running" && (
                      <Button
                        variant="secondary"
                        className="mt-3"
                        disabled={!access.user?.is_admin || cancelRun.isPending}
                        onClick={() => {
                          if (
                            window.confirm(
                              `确定取消本轮回归（#${summary.id}）吗？\n\n` +
                                `已完成的 ${summary.completed} 条结果会保留，未完成的任务会判失败，取消后不可继续。\n` +
                                `其他正在进行的回归不受影响。`,
                            )
                          ) {
                            cancelRun.mutate()
                          }
                        }}
                        data-testid="baseline-cancel-run"
                      >
                        {cancelRun.isPending ? "取消中…" : "取消本轮"}
                      </Button>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-3">
                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold">已完成</p>
                  <p className="font-data mt-2 text-lg font-bold">{selectedRun ? `${selectedRun.completed}/${selectedRun.total}` : "尚未运行"}</p>
                </div>
                {summary && (
                <div className="min-w-0 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-3" data-testid="baseline-confirmation-progress">
                  {(() => {
                    const progress = acceptanceSummary.data
                    const allRowsLoaded = progress?.run_id === summary.id && progress.total === summary.total
                    return (
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-xs font-semibold">逐条确认与纠偏</p>
                          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                            {!acceptanceReady
                              ? "运行结束后汇总全轮人工验收；当前展示进度和已完成结果"
                              : acceptanceSummary.isLoading
                              ? "正在汇总全轮人工验收进度"
                              : !progress || acceptanceSummary.isError
                              ? "人工验收进度读取失败，请刷新后重试"
                              : `已确认 ${progress.reviewed}/${progress.evaluable} · 未评分/失败阻塞 ${progress.blockers}`}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          disabled={!acceptanceReady || acceptanceSummary.isFetching || acceptanceSummary.isError || !allRowsLoaded || !progress?.complete}
                          onClick={() => toast.success("本轮人工验收条件已满足；当前仅展示本地完成摘要，不写入新状态。")}
                        >
                          完成人工验收
                        </Button>
                      </div>
                    )
                  })()}
                </div>
                )}
                <div className="min-w-0 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-3">
                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold">本轮完整工作流</p>
                  <p className="font-data mt-2 text-sm font-semibold">{summary?.selection.workflow ? workflowSelectionFacts(summary.selection.workflow).workflow : selectedRun ? "历史兼容回归" : "尚未运行"}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2">
                {selectedSet.data.runs.map((run) => (
                  <button
                    key={run.id}
                    type="button"
                    aria-pressed={run.id === selectedRunId}
                    className={"visual-native-button block " + (`min-w-44 border px-3 py-3 text-left ${
                      run.id === selectedRunId
                        ? "border-alias-edge-011 bg-alias-surface-016"
                        : "border-[var(--line)] bg-white hover:bg-alias-surface-051"
                    }`)}
                    onClick={() => selectRun(run.id, run.category_key)}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                      <span className="text-sm font-semibold">第 {run.sequence_no} 轮</span>
                      <Badge tone={statusTone(run)}>{statusName(run)}</Badge>
                    </div>
                    <p className="font-data mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                      {run.completed}/{run.total} · #<span className="visual-code">{run.id}</span>
                    </p>
                    <p className="font-data mt-1 truncate text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                      {run.selection.workflow
                        ? `评测机制 V${run.selection.workflow.version ?? "—"}`
                        : "历史兼容回归"}
                      {` · ${modelExecutionSummary(run.selection.model_execution_mode ?? "simulated").label}`}
                    </p>
                  </button>
                ))}
                {!selectedSet.data.runs.length && (
                  <p className="w-full border-y border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-7 text-center text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">
                    此基准集尚未运行。
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>
              <RunHistoryDrawer open={runHistoryDrawerOpen} onOpenChange={setRunHistoryDrawerOpen}>
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {historyDetails.isFetching && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">正在读取费用明细…</p>}
                  {historyDetails.isError && <p className="text-xs text-red-700">费用明细读取失败，请重新打开运行历史。</p>}
                  {selectedSet.data.runs.map((run) => (
                    <button
                      key={`history-${run.id}`}
                      type="button"
                      className="visual-native-button block w-full border border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-4 py-3 text-left hover:bg-alias-surface-051"
                      onClick={() => {
                        selectRun(run.id, run.category_key)
                        setRunHistoryDrawerOpen(false)
                      }}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                        <span className="text-sm font-semibold">第 {run.sequence_no} 轮</span>
                        <Badge tone={statusTone(run)}>{statusName(run)}</Badge>
                      </div>
                      <p className="font-data mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                        {run.completed}/{run.total} · #<span className="visual-code">{run.id}</span> · {modelExecutionSummary(run.selection.model_execution_mode ?? "simulated").label}
                      </p>
                      <RunCostEvidence cost={historyDetails.data?.runs.find(detail => detail.id === run.id)?.model_cost} />
                    </button>
                  ))}
                  {!selectedSet.data.runs.length && <p className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">此基准集尚未运行。</p>}
                </div>
              </RunHistoryDrawer>

              {summary && (
                <>
                {summary.workflow_runtime.selected
                  && (summary.workflow_runtime.failed > 0 || summary.workflow_runtime.canceled > 0) && (
                  <div className="mt-5 border-l-2 border-alias-edge-021 bg-alias-surface-067 px-4 py-4">
                    <p className="text-sm font-bold text-alias-content-041">工作流节点运行异常</p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-041">
                      节点执行失败 {summary.workflow_runtime.failed} 条，已取消 {summary.workflow_runtime.canceled} 条。
                      {summary.workflow_runtime.pending > 0 ? `仍在运行 ${summary.workflow_runtime.pending} 条。` : ""}
                      本轮完整机制未全部通过，请进入运行中心查看失败节点证据。
                    </p>
                  </div>
                )}
                {summary.workflow_runtime.selected
                  && summary.workflow_runtime.pending > 0
                  && summary.workflow_runtime.failed === 0
                  && summary.workflow_runtime.canceled === 0 && (
                  <div className="mt-5 border-l-2 border-alias-edge-011 bg-alias-surface-048 px-4 py-4">
                    <p className="text-sm font-bold">工作流节点仍在运行</p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                      已完成 {summary.workflow_runtime.succeeded}/{summary.workflow_runtime.total} 条节点流程。
                      全部节点结束后再进行本轮验收。
                    </p>
                  </div>
                )}
                {summary.status === "running" && (
                  <div className="mt-5 border-l-2 border-[var(--muted)] bg-alias-surface-038 px-4 py-4">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-sm font-bold">本轮回归进行中</p>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                          {summary.completed}/{summary.total} 已完成。只想停这一轮就用右侧按钮；
                          评测进度页的「取消全部」会把当时所有回归一并取消。
                        </p>
                      </div>

                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                <RunStepProgress run={summary} stepProgress={runDetail.data?.step_progress} />
                </>
              )}
              </Card>
              {summary && (
                <>
                {correctionItemId <= 0 && <RegressionDashboard
                  run={summary}
                  items={runDetail.data?.items ?? []}
                  fieldMetrics={fieldMetrics.data}
                  onOpenMetrics={() => setMetricsDrawerOpen(true)}
                />}
                <RegressionResults
                  run={summary}
                  stepProgress={runDetail.data?.step_progress}
                  items={runDetail.data?.items ?? []}
                  fullRunItems={acceptanceReady
                    ? acceptancePages.data?.flat() ?? runDetail.data?.items ?? []
                    : runDetail.data?.items ?? []}
                  pagination={runDetail.data?.pagination ?? {
                    offset: runPage * runPageSize,
                    limit: runPageSize,
                    total: summary.total,
                  }}
                  page={runPage}
                  onPageChange={setRunPage}
                  onPageSizeChange={size => {
                    if (![10, 25, 50].includes(size)) return
                    setRunPage(Math.floor(runPage * runPageSize / size))
                    setRunPageSize(size)
                  }}
                  loading={runDetail.isLoading || runDetail.isPlaceholderData}
                  fieldMetrics={fieldMetrics.data}
                  onPreview={setImagePreview}
                  onOpenMetrics={() => setMetricsDrawerOpen(true)}
                  correctionItemId={correctionItemId}
                  onOpenCorrection={openCorrection}
                  onCloseCorrection={closeCorrection}
                />
