                    <td className="px-4 py-3 visual-code">
                      {row.candidate_workflow_version_id ? `#${row.candidate_workflow_version_id}` : "—"}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <Badge tone={
                        row.status === "approved" ? "success"
                          : row.status === "failed" ? "danger"
                            : row.status === "awaiting_decision" ? "warning" : "neutral"
                      }>{statusLabel(row.status)}</Badge>
                      {row.status === "failed" && row.error_code && (
                        <p className="mt-1 visual-code text-[var(--muted)]">{row.error_code}</p>
                      )}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
                      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => setDetailId(row.id)}>查看详情</Button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
                {!(analyses.data?.items ?? []).length && !analyses.isLoading && (
                  <tr><td colSpan={6} className="px-5 py-10 text-center text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">
                    还没有发起过纠偏分析。在上面挑几个案例试试。
                  </td></tr>
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
          {analyses.data && analyses.data.total > 0 && (
            <div className="border-t border-[var(--line)]">
              <Pager page={analyses.data} fallbackLimit={PAGE_SIZE} onChange={setAnalysisOffset}
                     loading={analyses.isFetching} unit="次分析" />
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>

      <AnalysisDrawer
        analysisId={detailId}
        onOpenChange={(open) => { if (!open) setDetailId(null) }}
        onDecided={refresh}
      />
    </>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/pages/cover-regression-page.tsx =====
import { useRef, useState } from "react"
import { Link, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card"
import { api } from "@/lib/api"
import { useAccess } from "@/lib/access"
import { StockExportPanel } from "@/components/stock-export-panel"
import type { CoverRegressionDetail, CoverRegressionItem, CoverRegressionRun } from "@/lib/types"

const states: Record<string, string> = { freezing: "准备输入", prepared: "排队中", ready: "等待提交", uploading: "上传中", submitting: "正在核实提交",
  submitted: "已提交", queued: "网关排队", running: "生成中", downloading: "保存图片", download_pending: "等待下载图片", succeeded: "生成成功",
  failed: "生成失败", needs_review: "任务待核实", input_error: "输入不可用" }
const verdicts: Record<string, string> = { adopt: "可采用", regenerate: "需重新生成", uncertain: "无法判断" }

export function CoverRegressionPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useSearchParams()
  const setId = Number(search.get("set") || 0)
  const runId = search.get("run") || ""
  const [offset, setOffset] = useState(0)
  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState("将图中的主体完整呈现在白色背景上，保持其造型、材质和颜色，不添加文字或其他物品。")
  const [steps, setSteps] = useState(20)
  const [seed, setSeed] = useState(20261019)
  const [reviewing, setReviewing] = useState<CoverRegressionItem | null>(null)
  const [selectedAttempt, setSelectedAttempt] = useState("")
  const [verdict, setVerdict] = useState("adopt")
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("")
  const [resolving, setResolving] = useState<CoverRegressionItem | null>(null)
  const [evidence, setEvidence] = useState("")
  const launchIdentity = useRef({ signature: "", id: "" })
  const access = useAccess()
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const capabilities = useQuery({ queryKey: ["cover-capabilities"], queryFn: () => api<{ image_edit: boolean; text_to_image: boolean }>("/api/cover-regressions/capabilities") })
  const baseline = useQuery({ queryKey: ["cover-baseline", setId], enabled: setId > 0,
    queryFn: () => api<{ name: string; category_key: string; summary: { item_count: number } }>(`/api/baseline-sets/${setId}?include_items=false&summary_detail=overview`) })
  const rounds = useQuery({ queryKey: ["cover-rounds", setId],
    queryFn: () => api<{ items: CoverRegressionRun[] }>(`/api/cover-regressions${setId ? `?baseline_set_id=${setId}` : ""}`) })
  const detail = useQuery({ queryKey: ["cover-detail", runId, offset], enabled: !!runId, refetchInterval: 5000,
    queryFn: () => api<CoverRegressionDetail>(`/api/cover-regressions/${runId}?offset=${offset}&limit=24`) })
  const writable = access.can("reviews:write", detail.data?.category_key ?? baseline.data?.category_key)
  const refresh = () => { void client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["cover-detail"] }); void client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["cover-rounds"] }) }
  const mutation = useMutation({ mutationFn: ({ path, body }: { path: string; body?: unknown }) => api<unknown>(path, { method: "POST", ...(body ? { body: JSON.stringify(body) } : {}) }),
    onSuccess: () => { refresh(); setReviewing(null); setResolving(null) }, onError: (e: Error) => toast.error(e.message) })
  const create = useMutation({ mutationFn: () => {
    const signature = JSON.stringify({ setId, prompt, steps, seed })
    if (signature !== launchIdentity.current.signature) launchIdentity.current = { signature, id: `cover-${crypto.randomUUID()}` }
    return api<CoverRegressionRun>(`/api/baseline-sets/${setId}/cover-regressions`, { method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({ request_id: launchIdentity.current.id, prompt, width: 512, height: 512, steps, seed, cfg: 4 }) })
    }, onSuccess: (run) => { launchIdentity.current = { signature: "", id: "" }; setSearch({ set: String(setId), run: run.id }); setOffset(0); refresh() }, onError: (e: Error) => toast.error(e.message) })
  const imageUrl = (item: CoverRegressionItem, attempt: string) => `/api/cover-regressions/${runId}/items/${item.id}/attempts/${attempt}/image`
  return <>
    <PageHeader index="03" title="AI 生图存量回归" description="固定本轮样本、输入内容版本和生成参数，后台准备输入并逐条生成。人工复核可选，可只挑选少数图片纠偏。"
      actions={<>{runId && <StockExportPanel key={runId} source="cover" runId={runId} />}<Button variant="secondary" asChild><Link to="/workflow/optimization/baseline-regression">返回存量回归</Link></Button></>} />
    <div className="mx-auto shell-content space-y-6 px-page py-7 lg:py-10">
    <Card className="p-6 space-y-4">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">发起一轮改图</h2>
      <p>{baseline.data ? `${baseline.data.name} · ${baseline.data.summary.item_count} 条样本` : "请从存量回归页面选择基准集后进入，或查看下方已有轮次。"}</p>
      <label className="block space-y-2"><span>统一生成要求</span><textarea className="w-full rounded-xl border p-3" rows={3} value={prompt} onChange={e => setPrompt(e.target.value)} /></label>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-4">
        <label className="space-y-2"><span className="block">生成步数</span><input className="rounded-xl border p-3 w-28" type="number" min={1} max={40} value={steps} onChange={e => setSteps(Number(e.target.value))} /></label>
        <label className="space-y-2"><span className="block">随机种子</span><input className="rounded-xl border p-3 w-44" type="number" min={0} value={seed} onChange={e => setSeed(Number(e.target.value))} /></label>
        <p className="pb-3 text-muted-foreground">512 × 512 · 每条一张 · V100</p>
        <Button disabled={!setId || !writable || !capabilities.data?.image_edit || create.isPending || !prompt.trim()} onClick={() => create.mutate()}>{create.isPending ? "正在冻结样本…" : "确认样本与参数并开始"}</Button>
      </div>
      <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">20步历史实测约8分钟／张，400张约需54小时，实际受排队及运行情况影响。费用暂未核定。纯文字生图仍待接入验证。</p>
      {capabilities.data && !capabilities.data.image_edit && <p role="status">生图网关尚未配置，暂不能发起。</p>}
      {baseline.error && <p role="alert">{baseline.error.message}</p>}
    </Card>
    <section className="mt-6 space-y-4">
      <label className="flex items-center gap-3">查看轮次<select className="rounded-xl border bg-white p-3 min-w-0 max-w-full" value={runId} onChange={e => { setSearch({ ...(setId ? { set: String(setId) } : {}), run: e.target.value }); setOffset(0) }}>
        <option value="">请选择</option>{rounds.data?.items.map(run => <option key={run.id} value={run.id}>{run.name} · {run.created_at.slice(0, 16).replace("T", " ")}</option>)}
      </select></label>
      {(rounds.error || detail.error) && <p role="alert">{rounds.error?.message || detail.error?.message}</p>}
      {detail.data && <>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 items-center"><h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">共 {detail.data.total} 条 · {detail.data.status === "paused" ? "已暂停" : detail.data.status === "cancelled" ? "已取消" : detail.data.statistics.needs_review > 0 && detail.data.statistics.pending === 0 ? "等待核实任务" : detail.data.statistics.execution_finished ? "执行结束" : "后台处理中"}</h2>
          {["running", "paused"].includes(detail.data.status) && !detail.data.statistics.execution_finished && <><Button variant="secondary" disabled={!writable || mutation.isPending} onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ path: `/api/cover-regressions/${runId}/control/${detail.data!.status === "paused" ? "resume" : "pause"}` })}>{detail.data.status === "paused" ? "继续" : "暂停"}</Button>
            <Button variant="secondary" disabled={!writable || mutation.isPending} onClick={() => { if (window.confirm("取消后停止提交新任务，已提交的图片仍会保存。确认取消本轮？")) mutation.mutate({ path: `/api/cover-regressions/${runId}/control/cancel` }) }}>取消本轮</Button></>}
          <p className="text-muted-foreground">未人工复核不影响生成完成</p></div>
        {detail.data.status === "paused" && <p role="status">本轮已暂停。重新生成或重试会保留在队列中，点击“继续”后才提交；原任务查询与图片下载仍可进行。</p>}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-5 gap-3" aria-label="整轮进度">{[
          ["生成成功", detail.data.statistics.succeeded], ["失败或输入缺失", detail.data.statistics.failed],
          ["任务待核实", detail.data.statistics.needs_review], ["排队或在途", detail.data.statistics.pending],
          ["人工已复核", detail.data.statistics.reviewed],
        ].map(([label, count]) => <Card className="p-4" key={label}><p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{label}</p><p className="mt-2 text-xl font-semibold">{count}</p></Card>)}</div>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">已建立 {detail.data.statistics.generation_attempts} 次生成记录，网关确认受理 {detail.data.statistics.confirmed_jobs} 次，已保存 {detail.data.statistics.generated_images} 张生成图（包含历史版本）。费用未核定；未决提交不计入已确认受理次数。</p>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-5">{detail.data.items.map(item => <Card key={item.id} className="p-4 space-y-3 min-w-0">
          {item.effective_attempt_id ? <a href={imageUrl(item, item.effective_attempt_id)} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"><img className="w-full aspect-square object-contain bg-white rounded-xl" src={imageUrl(item, item.effective_attempt_id)} alt={item.name} loading="lazy" /></a> : <div className="aspect-square grid place-items-center rounded-xl bg-muted">{states[item.state] || item.state}</div>}
          <p className="break-all text-muted-foreground">{item.name}</p>
          <p>{states[item.state] || item.state} · {item.review ? `人工：${verdicts[item.review.verdict]}` : "未人工复核"}</p>
          {item.attempts[0] && <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">本次记录耗时（含排队）：{Math.floor(item.attempts[0].elapsed_seconds / 60)} 分 {item.attempts[0].elapsed_seconds % 60} 秒</p>}
          {item.review?.reason && <p className="break-words">{item.review.reason}</p>}
          {item.candidate_attempt_id && <div className="border-t pt-3 space-y-2"><p className="text-sm">有新生成候选，人工结论保持不变，待你确认采用。</p><a href={imageUrl(item,item.candidate_attempt_id)} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"><img className="h-28 w-full object-contain rounded-xl bg-white" src={imageUrl(item,item.candidate_attempt_id)} alt="新生成候选" /></a><Button variant="secondary" disabled={!writable} onClick={() => { setReviewing(item); setSelectedAttempt(item.candidate_attempt_id || ""); setVerdict("adopt"); setReason("") }}>复核新候选</Button></div>}
          {(item.input_error || item.attempts[0]?.error) && <p className="text-sm break-all">诊断：{item.input_error || item.attempts[0]?.error}</p>}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2"><Button variant="secondary" disabled={!writable || !item.effective_attempt_id} onClick={() => { setReviewing(item); setSelectedAttempt(item.effective_attempt_id || ""); setVerdict(item.review?.verdict || "adopt"); setReason(item.review?.reason || "") }}>人工纠偏</Button>
            {item.can_retry_input && <Button variant="secondary" disabled={!writable || mutation.isPending || detail.data!.status === "cancelled"} onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ path: `/api/cover-regressions/${runId}/items/${item.id}/retry-input` })}>重试准备输入</Button>}
            {item.attempts[0]?.can_recheck && <Button variant="secondary" disabled={!writable || mutation.isPending} onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ path: `/api/cover-regressions/${runId}/items/${item.id}/attempts/${item.attempts[0].id}/recheck` })}>查询原任务</Button>}
            {item.attempts[0]?.can_retry && <Button variant="secondary" disabled={!writable || mutation.isPending || detail.data!.status === "cancelled"} onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ path: `/api/cover-regressions/${runId}/items/${item.id}/attempts/${item.attempts[0].id}/retry` })}>重试此任务</Button>}
            {item.attempts[0]?.can_resolve && <Button variant="secondary" disabled={!writable || mutation.isPending} onClick={() => { setResolving(item); setEvidence("") }}>登记核实结论</Button>}
            <Button variant="secondary" disabled={!writable || mutation.isPending || !item.attempts[0]?.can_regenerate || detail.data!.status === "cancelled"} onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ path: `/api/cover-regressions/${runId}/items/${item.id}/regenerate` })}>重新生成</Button></div>
          {item.attempts[0]?.resolution && <p className="text-sm break-words">人工核实：{item.attempts[0].resolution.evidence}</p>}
        </Card>)}</div>
        <div className="flex gap-3 justify-end items-center"><span>{detail.data.total ? Math.floor(offset / 24) + 1 : 0} / {Math.ceil(detail.data.total / 24)} 页</span><Button variant="secondary" disabled={offset === 0} onClick={() => setOffset(Math.max(0, offset - 24))}>上一页</Button><Button variant="secondary" disabled={offset + 24 >= detail.data.total} onClick={() => setOffset(offset + 24)}>下一页</Button></div>
      </>}
    </section>
    {reviewing && <div role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="人工纠偏" className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/30 flex items-center justify-center p-4"><Card className="w-full max-w-lg p-6 space-y-4 max-h-[90vh] overflow-auto">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">人工纠偏</h2><p className="break-all">{reviewing.name}</p>
      <label className="block">图片版本<select className="block w-full rounded-xl border p-3 mt-2" value={selectedAttempt} onChange={e => setSelectedAttempt(e.target.value)}>{reviewing.attempts.filter(a => a.state === "succeeded").map(a => <option key={a.id} value={a.id}>第 {a.sequence} 次生成</option>)}</select></label>
      {selectedAttempt && <img className="w-full h-48 object-contain" src={imageUrl(reviewing, selectedAttempt)} alt="选中的图片版本" />}
      <label className="block">结论<select className="block w-full rounded-xl border p-3 mt-2" value={verdict} onChange={e => setVerdict(e.target.value)}>{Object.entries(verdicts).map(([key, label]) => <option key={key} value={key}>{label}</option>)}</select></label>
      <label className="block">说明<textarea className="block w-full rounded-xl border p-3 mt-2" value={reason} onChange={e => setReason(e.target.value)} maxLength={4000} /></label>
      <div className="flex gap-3 justify-end"><Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setReviewing(null)}>取消</Button><Button disabled={mutation.isPending} onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ path: `/api/cover-regressions/${runId}/items/${reviewing.id}/review`, body: { attempt_id: selectedAttempt, expected_revision: reviewing.review?.revision || 0, verdict, reason } })}>确认修改</Button></div>
    </Card></div>}
    {resolving && <div role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="登记任务核实结论" className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/30 flex items-center justify-center p-4"><Card className="w-full max-w-lg p-6 space-y-4">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">登记任务核实结论</h2><p>请先核对网关队列与执行记录，确认原任务已结束或未被受理。仅超时、查询不到记录，都不足以确认。</p><p>确认后将本次尝试记为失败并释放排队占用，保留原记录，不会立即重新生成。</p>
      <label className="block">核实依据<textarea className="block w-full rounded-xl border p-3 mt-2" value={evidence} maxLength={2000} onChange={e => setEvidence(e.target.value)} /></label>
      <div className="flex gap-3 justify-end"><Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setResolving(null)}>取消</Button><Button disabled={mutation.isPending || !evidence.trim()} onClick={() => mutation.mutate({ path: `/api/cover-regressions/${runId}/items/${resolving.id}/attempts/${resolving.attempts[0].id}/resolve`, body: { evidence, expected_updated_at: resolving.attempts[0].updated_at, confirmed: true } })}>确认原任务已结束</Button></div>
    </Card></div>}
    </div>
  </>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/pages/daily-review-page.tsx =====
import {
  ArrowClockwise,
  CheckCircle,
  Clock,
  PauseCircle,
  Play,
  ShieldWarning,
  UserFocus,
} from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { useMemo, useState } from "react"
import { Link } from "react-router-dom"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { reviewCapacityApi } from "@/lib/api"
import type { DailyReviewCapacity } from "@/lib/types"

const categoryLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  knowledge_graph: "知识图谱",
  inspiration_image: "灵感图",
  model_3d_su: "3D & SU",
  space_image: "空间图片",
  material_image: "材质图",
}

const bucketCopy = {
  risk_error: { label: "风险与异常", note: "优先确认错误、失败和高风险结论" },
  cold_start_drift: { label: "新机制与变化", note: "观察新版本和结果变化" },
  stratified_random: { label: "分层抽查", note: "覆盖不同类目、机制和结果" },
  golden_sentinel: { label: "黄金样本哨兵", note: "持续检查已确认标准是否稳定" },
} as const

function categoryLabel(key: string) {
  return categoryLabels[key] ?? key.replaceAll("_", " ")
}

function waitLabel(seconds: number) {
  if (seconds <= 0) return "没有等待"
  if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds} 秒`
  if (seconds < 3600) return `${Math.ceil(seconds / 60)} 分钟`
  return `${Math.ceil(seconds / 3600)} 小时`
}

function bucketTone(key: DailyReviewCapacity["buckets"][number]["key"]) {
  if (key === "risk_error") return "danger" as const
  if (key === "cold_start_drift") return "warning" as const
  if (key === "golden_sentinel") return "success" as const
  return "neutral" as const
}

export function DailyReviewPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const [resumePauseId, setResumePauseId] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const [resumeReason, setResumeReason] = useState("")
  const capacity = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["daily-review-capacity", "today"],
    queryFn: reviewCapacityApi.today,
    refetchInterval: 15_000,
  })
  const generate = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => reviewCapacityApi.generate(capacity.data?.business_date ?? ""),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      toast.success("今日抽查批次已生成")
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["daily-review-capacity"] })
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })
  const resume = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ pauseId, reason }: { pauseId: number; reason: string }) => (
      reviewCapacityApi.resume(pauseId, reason)
    ),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      toast.success("增量评测已恢复，操作原因已记录")
      setResumePauseId(null)
      setResumeReason("")
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["daily-review-capacity"] })
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })
  const data = capacity.data
  const pendingItems = useMemo(
    () => (data?.items ?? []).filter((item) => item.selection_state === "selected_pending"),
    [data?.items],
  )
  const reviewedItems = useMemo(
    () => (data?.items ?? []).filter((item) => item.selection_state === "selected_reviewed"),
    [data?.items],
  )
  const completion = data?.batch?.selected_count
    ? Math.round((data.progress.reviewed / data.batch.selected_count) * 100)
    : 0

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        index="02.5"
        title="今日人审"
        description="每天从所有增量评测中抽出最多 100 条给人工确认。未被抽中的结果只记为“未抽查”，不会被当成人工通过。"
        actions={(
          <>
            <Badge tone={data?.pauses.length ? "warning" : "success"}>
              {data?.pauses.length ? `${data.pauses.length} 条链路已暂停` : "增量抽查正常"}
            </Badge>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => capacity.refetch()} disabled={capacity.isFetching}>
              <ArrowClockwise />刷新
            </Button>
          </>
        )}
      />
      <div className="mx-auto shell-content min-w-0 px-page space-y-6 py-7 lg:py-9">
        <section className="visual-panel-group grid gap-px visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
          <Metric label="今日人审容量" value={`${data?.capacity ?? 100} 条`} note={`${data?.reviewer_count ?? 1} 人负责`} />
          <Metric label="待审核" value={`${data?.progress.pending ?? 0} 条`} note={`已完成 ${completion}%`} />
          <Metric label="已审核" value={`${data?.progress.reviewed ?? 0} 条`} note="以人工提交事实为准" />
          <Metric label="未抽查" value={`${(data?.overflow.ordinary ?? 0) + (data?.overflow.severe ?? 0)} 条`} note="不代表人工通过" />
          <Metric label="最长等待" value={waitLabel(data?.oldest_wait_seconds ?? 0)} note={data?.business_date ?? "读取中"} />
        </section>

        {!data?.batch && !capacity.isLoading && (
          <section className="visual-panel-group grid gap-px visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-[var(--line)] lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_360px]">
            <div className="bg-white px-5 py-7 md:px-6">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><UserFocus size={22} /><h2 className="visual-card-title">今天还没有正式抽查批次</h2></div>
              <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">生成后顺序固定，同一天重复点击不会重复建批或改变抽查名单。</p>
            </div>
            <aside className="bg-active-surface px-5 py-7 md:px-6">
              <Button className="w-full" onClick={() => generate.mutate()} disabled={generate.isPending || !data?.business_date}>
                <Play weight="fill" />生成今日抽查
              </Button>
            </aside>
          </section>
        )}

        <section className="rounded-lg shadow-card border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4 px-5 py-5 md:px-6">
            <div><p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">抽查构成</p><h2 className="visual-card-title mt-1">四类样本按优先级进入今日名单</h2></div>
            <Badge>{data?.batch ? `共抽中 ${data.batch.selected_count} 条` : "等待生成"}</Badge>
          </div>
          <div className="visual-panel-edge grid gap-px border-t border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
            {(data?.buckets ?? []).map((bucket) => (
              <div key={bucket.key} className="bg-white px-5 py-5">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3"><p className="text-sm font-bold">{bucketCopy[bucket.key].label}</p><Badge tone={bucketTone(bucket.key)}>{bucket.selected}/{bucket.capacity}</Badge></div>
                <div className="mt-4 h-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-surface-sunken"><div className="h-full bg-primary" style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, bucket.capacity ? (bucket.selected / bucket.capacity) * 100 : 0)}%` }} /></div>
                <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{bucketCopy[bucket.key].note}</p>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>

        {(data?.pauses.length ?? 0) > 0 && (
          <section className="border-y border-danger bg-danger-wash">
            <div className="flex items-start gap-3 px-5 py-5 md:px-6"><PauseCircle className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-danger" size={24} weight="fill" /><div><h2 className="visual-card-title">有增量链路已暂停</h2><p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">严重风险超过今天的人审容量，平台已停止对应类目和评测版本继续写入下游；其他链路不受影响。</p></div></div>
            <div className="border-t border-danger-wash">
              {data?.pauses.map((pause) => (
                <div key={pause.id} className="grid gap-4 border-b border-danger-wash px-5 py-5 last:border-0 md:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_360px] md:px-6">
                  <div><div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><strong>{categoryLabel(pause.category_key)}</strong><Badge tone="danger">已暂停</Badge></div><p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">{pause.reason_label}</p><details className="visual-native-details mt-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]"><summary className="cursor-pointer font-semibold">查看评测版本</summary><p className="mt-2 visual-code">{pause.mechanism_key || "当前类目全部版本"}</p></details></div>
                  <div>
                    {resumePauseId === pause.id ? (
                      <div className="space-y-2"><label className="text-xs font-bold" htmlFor={`resume-${pause.id}`}>恢复原因</label><textarea id={`resume-${pause.id}`} className="visual-native-textarea min-h-20 w-full" value={resumeReason} onChange={(event) => setResumeReason(event.target.value)} placeholder="说明已经处理了什么风险" /><div className="flex gap-2"><Button size="sm" onClick={() => resume.mutate({ pauseId: pause.id, reason: resumeReason })} disabled={!resumeReason.trim() || resume.isPending}>确认恢复</Button><Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => setResumePauseId(null)}>取消</Button></div></div>
                    ) : <Button variant="secondary" className="w-full" onClick={() => setResumePauseId(pause.id)}>恢复并记录原因</Button>}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        <section className="visual-panel-group grid gap-px visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-[var(--line)] xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.65fr)_minmax(300px,0.35fr)]">
          <div className="bg-white">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-5 md:px-6"><div><p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">主要操作</p><h2 className="visual-card-title mt-1">按顺序确认今日待审核内容</h2></div><Badge tone={pendingItems.length ? "warning" : "success"}>{pendingItems.length ? `${pendingItems.length} 条待处理` : "今日已完成"}</Badge></div>
            <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)]">
              {pendingItems.slice(0, 100).map((item) => (
                <div key={item.id} className="grid items-center gap-3 px-5 py-4 text-sm md:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_150px_140px_auto] md:px-6">
                  <div><p className="font-bold">{categoryLabel(item.category_key)}</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">今日第 {item.stable_rank} 条 · {item.bucket_label}</p></div><Badge tone={bucketTone(item.bucket)}>{item.bucket_label}</Badge><span className="text-xs font-semibold">{item.state_label}</span>{item.evaluation_id ? <Button asChild size="sm" variant="secondary"><Link to={`/workflow/review/low-confidence?evaluation=${item.evaluation_id}`}>开始审核</Link></Button> : <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled>等待结果接入</Button>}
                  <details className="visual-native-details md:col-span-4 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]"><summary className="cursor-pointer">查看来源详情</summary><p className="mt-2 visual-code">{item.source_result_key}</p></details>
                </div>
              ))}
              {!pendingItems.length && <div className="px-5 py-12 text-center"><CheckCircle className="mx-auto text-success" size={32} weight="fill" /><p className="mt-3 text-sm font-bold">当前没有待审核内容</p><p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">稍后刷新即可查看新进度。</p></div>}
            </div>
          </div>
          <aside className="bg-active-surface px-5 py-6 md:px-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><ShieldWarning size={20} /><h2 className="visual-card-title">结果含义</h2></div>
            <dl className="mt-5 space-y-5 text-sm"><div><dt className="font-bold">待审核</dt><dd className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">已进入今天正式名单，等待人工提交。</dd></div><div><dt className="font-bold">已审核</dt><dd className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">已经有人工确认事实，共 {reviewedItems.length} 条。</dd></div><div><dt className="font-bold">未抽查</dt><dd className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">今天容量之外的候选，不等于人工通过。</dd></div></dl>
            <div className="mt-6 border-t border-active-border pt-5"><div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Clock size={18} /><p className="text-xs font-bold">本页自动刷新</p></div><p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">每 15 秒更新一次，不会改变已固定的名单顺序。</p></div>
          </aside>
        </section>
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

function Metric({ label, value, note }: { label: string; value: string; note: string }) {
  return <div className="bg-white px-5 py-4"><p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p><p className="mt-2 font-data text-2xl font-bold">{value}</p><p className="mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{note}</p></div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/pages/dashboard-page.tsx =====
import { ArrowRight, CheckCircle, CircleNotch, Images, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { Link } from "react-router-dom"

import { HoverPreview, ImageSurface } from "@/components/hover-preview"
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/app-shell"
import { MotionRegion } from "@/components/motion/motion-region"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Card, CardHeader } from "@/components/ui/card"
import { EmptyState, ErrorState, Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/async-state"
import { Progress } from "@/components/ui/progress"
import { failureApi } from "@/features/failure-console/api"
import { api } from "@/lib/api"
import { runtimeApi } from "@/lib/runtime-api"
import type { Asset, Dashboard } from "@/lib/types"
import { formatPlatformDate } from "../lib/platform-time"

const levelOrder = ["L5", "L4", "L3", "L2", "L1"] as const
const levelColors = { L1: "text-level-1", L2: "text-level-2", L3: "text-level-3", L4: "text-level-4", L5: "text-level-5" }

export function DashboardPage() {
  const dashboard = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["dashboard"],
    queryFn: () => api<Dashboard>("/api/dashboard"),
    refetchInterval: 15000,
  })
  const assets = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["assets", "recent"],
    queryFn: () => api<{ items: Asset[] }>("/api/assets?limit=6"),
  })
  const failures = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["failures", "dashboard"],
    queryFn: () => failureApi.list({ resolved: false, limit: 1 }),
    refetchInterval: 15000,
  })
  const capacity = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["runtime-capacity", "dashboard"],
    queryFn: runtimeApi.getCapacity,
    refetchInterval: 15000,
  })
  const data = dashboard.data
  const dashboardLoading = dashboard.isLoading && !data
  const totalEvaluated = Object.values(data?.levels ?? {}).reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0)

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        index="01"
        title="评测总览"
        description="查看当前异常待办、素材处理进度和模型配置。"
        actions={<Button asChild><Link to="/assets">上传图片<ArrowRight weight="bold" /></Link></Button>}
      />
      <MotionRegion className="mx-auto shell-content px-page pb-page" visibleItems={5}>
        <div data-dashboard-content="" className="grid min-w-0 gap-card-gap lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.2fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
          <div className="min-w-0 space-y-card-gap">
            <Card data-dashboard-card="todo" className="min-w-0">
              <CardHeader title={<span className="flex items-center gap-3"><WarningCircle size={24} aria-hidden="true" />当前异常待办</span>} />
              <div className="grid min-w-0 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2">
                {[
                  { field: "failures", label: "评测失败", value: failures.isError ? undefined : failures.data?.total,
                    note: "增量评测 · 尚未处理", empty: "暂无待处理的评测失败", error: failures.isError,
                    to: "/workflow/failures", action: "处理评测失败" },
                  { field: "outbox", label: "投递异常", value: capacity.isError ? undefined : capacity.data?.dead_letters.outbox,
                    note: "下游投递 · 已停止自动重试", empty: "暂无待处理的投递异常", error: capacity.isError,
                    to: "/workflow/operations/detail?tab=processing&legacy=1#dead-letters", action: "处理投递异常" },
                ].map(({ field, label, value, note, empty, error, to, action }) => <div key={field} className="min-w-0">
                  <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-2">{label}</p>
                  <p className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-3">
                    <span data-dashboard-number={field} className="visual-large-number min-w-0 break-all" style={{ fontSize: 56 }}>{value ?? "—"}</span>
                    <span className="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">条</span>
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground" role={error ? "alert" : undefined}>
                    {error ? "读取失败，请稍后刷新" : value === undefined ? "正在读取" : value === 0 ? empty : note}
                  </p>
                  <Button asChild variant="ghost" className="mt-3"><Link to={to}>{action}<ArrowRight /></Link></Button>
                </div>)}
              </div>
            </Card>

            <Card data-dashboard-card="processing" className="min-w-0">
              <CardHeader title="处理进度" description="每 15 秒刷新一次" />
              {dashboard.isError && <ErrorState title={<span className="flex items-center gap-2"><WarningCircle size={18} aria-hidden="true" />评测总览加载失败</span>}
                description={dashboard.error.message} className="py-0 mb-4" />}
              <dl className="grid min-w-0 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-3">
                {[
                  { field: "queued", label: "等待评测", value: data?.queued, note: "原生素材运行", icon: CircleNotch },
                  { field: "processing", label: "正在处理", value: data?.processing, note: "原生素材运行", icon: CircleNotch },
                  { field: "asset_count", label: "素材总数", value: data?.asset_count, note: "平台素材库", icon: Images },
                ].map(({ field, label, value, note, icon: Icon }) => <div key={field} className="min-w-0">
                  <dt className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] text-ink-2"><Icon size={18} aria-hidden="true" />{label}</dt>
                  <dd data-dashboard-number={field} className="visual-large-number mt-4 break-all" style={{ fontSize: 40 }}>{value ?? "—"}</dd>
                  <dd className="mt-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{dashboardLoading ? "正在汇总" : note}</dd>
                </div>)}
              </dl>
            </Card>

            <Card data-dashboard-card="levels" className="min-w-0">
              <CardHeader title="等级分布" description="只用于监控，不强制固定比例" />
              <div className="space-y-5">
                {levelOrder.map(level => {
                  const count = data ? data.levels[level] ?? 0 : null
                  // max=0 is unknown in Progress: keep the real zero count in the
                  // label, show an empty track and — ratio, never invent max=1.
                  return <div key={level} data-dashboard-level={level}>
                    <Progress label={`${level} · ${count ?? "—"} 张`} value={count} max={totalEvaluated} className={levelColors[level]} />
                  </div>
                })}
              </div>
            </Card>

          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0 space-y-card-gap">
            <Card data-dashboard-card="recent" className="min-w-0">
              <CardHeader title="最近素材" description="最新上传的原始图片"
                actions={<Button asChild variant="ghost" size="sm"><Link to="/assets">查看全部<ArrowRight /></Link></Button>} />
              {assets.isLoading ? <div aria-label="正在读取最近素材" className="grid gap-card-gap sm:grid-cols-2">
                {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, index) => <Skeleton key={index} className="h-40" />)}
              </div> : assets.isError ? <ErrorState title={<span className="flex items-center gap-2"><WarningCircle size={18} aria-hidden="true" />最近素材加载失败</span>} description={assets.error.message} /> : assets.data?.items.length ? (
                <div className="grid min-w-0 gap-card-gap sm:grid-cols-2">
                  {assets.data.items.map(asset => <div key={asset.id} data-dashboard-asset={asset.id} className="min-w-0">
                    <HoverPreview src={asset.image_url} alt={asset.name} className="block [&>[data-image-surface]]:w-full">
                      <ImageSurface src={asset.image_url} alt="" className="h-40 w-full" />
                    </HoverPreview>
                    <p className="file-name mt-3 break-words text-[13px]">{asset.name}</p>
                    <div className="font-data mt-2 flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                      <p data-dashboard-dimensions="">{asset.width} × {asset.height}</p>
                      <time dateTime={asset.created_at}>{formatPlatformDate(asset.created_at)}</time>
                    </div>
                  </div>)}
                </div>
              ) : <EmptyState title={<span className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3"><Images size={28} aria-hidden="true" />还没有素材</span>}
                description="上传第一批空间或建筑图片，系统会建立可追溯的评测任务。" className="text-center" />}
            </Card>
            <Card data-dashboard-card="model" className="min-w-0 self-start">
              <CardHeader title="模型状态" />
              <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                {dashboardLoading ? <CircleNotch size={24} className="shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" /> : data ? (
                  data.model.has_api_key ? <CheckCircle size={24} className="shrink-0 text-success" weight="fill" aria-hidden="true" /> : <WarningCircle size={24} className="shrink-0 text-warning" aria-hidden="true" />
                ) : <WarningCircle size={24} className="shrink-0 text-danger" aria-hidden="true" />}
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  {data ? <>
                    <p className="break-words text-[15px] font-semibold">{data.model.name}</p>
                    <p className="font-data mt-2 break-all text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">{data.model.model_id || "尚未配置模型端点"}</p>
                    <Badge className="mt-4" tone={data.model.has_api_key ? "success" : "warning"}>{data.model.has_api_key ? "已配置密钥" : "需要配置"}</Badge>
                  </> : dashboardLoading ? <>
                    <p className="text-[15px] font-semibold">正在汇总模型状态</p>
                    <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">请稍候</p>
                  </> : <ErrorState title="模型状态未能读取" description={dashboard.error?.message} className="py-0" />}
                </div>
              </div>
            </Card>

          </div>
        </div>
      </MotionRegion>
    </>
  )
}
